## `AFG-4921-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Catenary-Laser-Geometry Metrologist

Minimum approach distance to catenary is 1.5 m. Work zone boundary at least 2.0 m f…
